review_id: 7936
entity_slug: lamberts-cafe-llDIlI

rating: 5
review_text: It's been a decade since I last ate here. I always recommend this place to tourists or friends/family visiting. That wait line can be ridiculous! Had lunch here on Saturday just in time to see a bus drop off 50 or more people. Time for that hour wait. My group consisted of six plus a baby. My sister was visiting from LA, so had to take her here. We were seated after nearly an hour wait and I ordered the county fried steak w/green beans and cucumber/onions. Also a lemonade. The food and pass arounds were fabulous. Rolls stupendous. Every one caught a roll, but my sister got hit on her head and caught it afterwards. Recorded the whole thing and it's as funny now to me as when it happened! Also slit two cinnamon rolls for the table. Only two complaints from the table to report. Bacon cheeseburger was bland and the ribeye steak while cooked correctly, had more char than desirable and some scrapping was needed. Service was excellent, we took lots of pictures and the staff graciously took them for us to remember the occasion. Lambert's Cafe remains an institution,and I stand by that. I paid for lunch, and six people was a solid $200+ bill after the tip. Parking was great too and not a lot of traffic to deal with. Truely a fantastic lunch... that wait and that bill does not invite local dinning. I'll gladly take friends/family who are visiting for a one-time experience so it might be another decade before I return.  BTW, also bought souvenirs from the gift shop but I didn't include that in that lunch cost.

Fried Okra, black eyed peas, collard greens, macaroni and cheese with tomato sauce, apple butter and molasses. It more like a experience! Been in business since 1942 Hometown Favorite. Tourist spot, also has on site Souvenir store. Not far from Gulf Shores and Orange Beach.
Fun for the whole family.
Meals were delicious as well. You will leave with a full belly! It was a adventure for sure. We will definitely visit again when ever we are in the area.

--- entity_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
id: d44f8934-a35a-4024-a487-21fa451e6eb3
entity_slug: lamberts-cafe-llDIlI
reviewer_name: ricecube348
rating: 5
body: It's been a decade since I last ate here. I always recommend this place to tourists or friends/family visiting. That wait line can be ridiculous! Had lunch here on Saturday just in time to see a bus drop off 50 or more people. Time for that hour wait. My group consisted of six plus a baby. My sister was visiting from LA, so had to take her here. We were seated after nearly an hour wait and I ordered the county fried steak w/green beans and cucumber/onions. Also a lemonade. The food and pass arounds were fabulous. Rolls stupendous. Every one caught a roll, but my sister got hit on her head and caught it afterwards. Recorded the whole thing and it's as funny now to me as when it happened! Also slit two cinnamon rolls for the table. Only two complaints from the table to report. Bacon cheeseburger was bland and the ribeye steak while cooked correctly, had more char than desirable and some scrapping was needed. Service was excellent, we took lots of pictures and the staff graciously took them for us to remember the occasion. Lambert's Cafe remains an institution,and I stand by that. I paid for lunch, and six people was a solid $200+ bill after the tip. Parking was great too and not a lot of traffic to deal with. Truely a fantastic lunch... that wait and that bill does not invite local dinning. I'll gladly take friends/family who are visiting for a one-time experience so it might be another decade before I return.  BTW, also bought souvenirs from the gift shop but I didn't include that in that lunch cost.
